Self-test `Test-LlmDelegateStdinHandlingSelfTest.ps1` **extended** (not recreated — it already proved `-MessagePath` for opencode, case C): a static guard by **independent tokens** (`-MessagePath` in `FromFile`, `Get-Content -LiteralPath … -Encoding utf8`, `Test-Path -LiteralPath … -PathType Leaf`) across the 6 adapters + an assertion of the size guard in the argument-based ones; and a **dedicated behavioral** test of `-MessagePath` on `Invoke-Codex` (a fake-exe via `-CodexExe` that writes **raw text** to the `-o output-last-message`, proving the `-MessagePath → $Message → stdin → -o → return` chain, distinct from opencode's >32KB case C). **No effect** on the confidentiality gate (`-MessagePath` changes the origin, not the destination). Documented in `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md` (the 6 adapter pointers + the self-test entry) and `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md` (SCRIPTS signatures + the «ANTI-HANG DE STDIN HEADLESS» and «LIMITE 32KB» sections, whose follow-up line went stale). Plan converged via **peer review** (4 rounds v1→v4, 5 reviewers / 3 families — native anthropic, openai/Codex gpt-5.5, ollama-cloud deepseek-v4-pro/glm-5.2/kimi-k2.7-code; v4 approved 5/5; the inclusion of the Claude Code adapters was a Codex/glm finding, the redesign of the Codex behavioral test and the size guard were glm findings).
- `scripts/Invoke-OpenCode.ps1` gained **`-MaxAttempts <1-3>` (default 1 = historical behavior): opt-in retry-once** against the **intermittent, rare** truncation of ollama-cloud "coder" voices (`kimi-k2.7-code`/`minimax-m3`) — surviving hypothesis = **tail non-determinism** (the model ends after a tool-call without the final `stop`); empirically **not** quota, timeout, step budget, or a fixed model property (not reproduced across ~8 calls). With 2+, it re-dispatches **only** a `truncated`/`no-completion` verdict. Per-attempt precedence: (1) timeout/exit≠0/explicit-stream-error → terminal; (2) completion verdict → retries only `{truncated,no-completion}` (`empty` terminal); (3) **when deciding to retry**, it checks for a **429 in the attempt window** (`Get-OpenCodeUsageLimitError`, `$startedAt` reassigned per iteration) → if present, terminal (do not re-burn the weekly quota). With no pending retry (default `-MaxAttempts 1` or the last attempt), the reported verdict is the completion one (e.g., `truncated`). `empty` (clean stop with no text) is **terminal**; `-TimeoutSec` is **per attempt** (with `-MaxAttempts 2` wall time can double); `-Raw` does **not** retry; each retry emits `OPENCODE_RETRY: attempt=N status=… reason=…` on stderr and the final throw message includes the last attempt's status. **Sync-only** (the async `Start-`/`Watch-OpenCodeJob` suffers the same truncation but has no retry — follow-up in `999`). New self-test `scripts/Test-OpenCodeRetrySelfTest.ps1` (token `OK: Test-OpenCodeRetrySelfTest.ps1`): fake-exe with a **file-based counter** + an `XDG_DATA_HOME` seam for the 429 case (cases i–v). Documented in `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md` (note next to Finding D), `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`, and `15-revisao-por-pares.md:83` (qualifies the generic `-Cd` use: the opencode adapter has **no** `-Cd`). **Security finding documented** (new section «LIMITE CONHECIDO — OPENCODE: AGENTE DEFAULT AUTO-APROVA FERRAMENTAS LOCAIS EM HEADLESS» in `SKILL.md`): in headless `opencode run` the default `build` agent (and `--agent plan`) auto-approves `bash`, so the panel grants **local execution** to an external model; the confidentiality gate governs the **send**, not **local execution** (distinct axes); `public→allow` is automatic and **does not pass through a human node**; mitigation = `-Agent` with a custom agent `permission: bash: deny` (the `read` tool stays unrestricted — its own front in `999`). Plan converged via **peer review** (v1→v5, 5 voices / 3 families: native anthropic, openai/Codex gpt-5.5, ollama-cloud deepseek-v4-pro/glm-5.2/kimi-k2.7-code).
- `scripts/Invoke-OpenCode.ps1` now **diagnoses the provider usage limit (HTTP 429) on timeout** instead of masking it as "excedeu Xs". opencode retries the 429 **silently** (stdout/stderr stay empty, confirmed up to 180s) and records the error only in its **own log** (`~/.local/share/opencode/log/`, honoring `XDG_DATA_HOME`). New helper `Get-OpenCodeUsageLimitError` in `scripts/OpenCodeStreamSupport.ps1` (dot-sourced; `-LogDir` for fixtures) scans the process-window log for `"statusCode":429` + the limit message; on the timeout branch the wrapper throws a clear error ("provider usage limit (HTTP 429)... wait for the cycle reset; e.g., ollama-cloud weekly usage limit") when a 429 is present, otherwise keeps the "excedeu Xs". Reason: in a reinforced pre-push, 3 `ollama-cloud` models "timed out" with no visible cause — it was the **weekly quota** exhausted; the masked diagnostic cost hours. Validated **live** against the real 429 + a fixture self-test `scripts/Test-OpenCodeUsageLimitDetectionSelfTest.ps1` (token `OPENCODE_USAGE_LIMIT_DETECTION_SELFTEST_OK`). Extending it to the sibling adapters (`Start-OpenCodeJob`/`Watch-OpenCodeJob`, Codex/Gemini/Copilot) is a follow-up in `999`. Documented in `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Deploy-bin documentation precision (**pre-existing** gap, surfaced in the reinforced pre-push review of the `.ContainsKey` front; **no code change**): `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md:190` described `Test-GeneXusDeployBinFreshness.ps1` as a gate (`-PostImportDeployValidation`/`-BuildOperationallySucceeded`/exit 49), but that script is a **read-only diagnostic**; the policy/gate (exit 49) lives in `GeneXusKbDeployBinSupport.ps1` (`Resolve-GeneXusKbDeployBinCheckPolicy`). Reassigned: `:189` (support-library) gained the policy + `Test-GeneXusDeployBinPolicySelfTest.ps1` + exit 49; `:190` became the read-only diagnostic with its real params. And `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md` gained a bullet naming the JSON deploy-bin signals (`deployBinFreshness`, `deployBinCheck.binCheck.publicationFreshSinceBuild`/`objectDllMaxWriteTime`/`configMaxWriteTime`) and the rule **not** to declare deploy OK with `stale`/exit 49. (The `.ContainsKey` entry below said "`08` reviewed, no edit needed" for **that** change; this is an **independent** `08`/`09` gap fix.)
- Fixed a **masked-diagnostic** bug in the BuildAll (and SpecifyGenerate) post-processing under `Set-StrictMode -Version Latest`: `scripts/GeneXusKbDeployBinSupport.ps1:482` called `.ContainsKey('sentinelFreshSinceBuild')` on `$freshness.binCheck`, which is an `[ordered]@{}` — `OrderedDictionary` does not expose `.ContainsKey`, only `.Contains`. On the fresh .NET Core publication path (`status='fresh'`), the exception propagated to the outer catch of `scripts/Invoke-GeneXusKbBuildAll.ps1:2206` (status at `:2208`; analogous in `scripts/Invoke-GeneXusKbSpecifyGenerate.ps1:1716`), downgrading the diagnostic to `compilou limpo com falha no pos-processamento` with `postProcessingError` set, even though the build was clean (`msBuildExitCode=0`). **Fix A:** `.ContainsKey`→`.Contains` (`:482`). **Fix B (found in review):** `Add-GeneXusKbDeployBinPublicationFieldsToBinCheck` declared the parameter as `[hashtable]$BinCheck` (`:250`), but the single call site (`:357`) passes the `[ordered]@{}` — passing an `OrderedDictionary` to a `[hashtable]` parameter **creates a copy**, so the 11 publication fields never reached the reported `binCheck`; changed to `[System.Collections.IDictionary]$BinCheck` (accepts the `OrderedDictionary` by reference, preserving order). This **aligns the output with an already-published contract**: `scripts/msbuild-exit-codes.catalog.json` (exit 49) already lists **some of these fields** (`publicationFreshSinceBuild`, `objectDllMaxWriteTime`, `configMaxWriteTime`) in `jsonHints` — those three promised and silently suppressed, now actually present in `deployBinCheck.binCheck`. Both consumers (`Invoke-GeneXusKbBuildAll.ps1:2016` and `Invoke-GeneXusKbSpecifyGenerate.ps1:1533`) are fixed by the same shared function. New self-test `scripts/Test-GeneXusDeployBinClassificationSelfTest.ps1` (token `GENEXUS_DEPLOY_BIN_CLASSIFICATION_SELFTEST_OK`): temporary `dotnet-core-self-host` KB, timestamps pinned via `LastWriteTime`, assertions on `deployBinCheck.binCheck` (publication **and** sentinel fields) and `warnings` — fails without Fix A (throws) and without Fix B (publication fields missing). Repo-wide sweep: `:482` is the only `.ContainsKey` over `[ordered]`; `.ContainsValue`=0 occurrences; `.Remove`/`.Add` exist on `OrderedDictionary` (safe). Documented in `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`; `xpz-msbuild-build/SKILL.md`, `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md` and the exit-code catalog **reviewed, no edit needed**. Plan converged via **peer review** (5 versions, 4 voices / 3 families — native anthropic, openai/Codex gpt-5.5, ollama-cloud glm-5.2/deepseek-v4-pro).
- Taught `scripts/Test-XpzWrapperInventory.ps1` to flag `INVENTORY_CUSTOMIZED` with the **generic reason `forwards_unknown_engine_param`** (new helper `scripts/XpzWrapperEngineParamSupport.ps1`, dot-sourced): via AST + `Get-Command` (without running the engine, without a KB) it verifies that every parameter a local wrapper **forwards** to a shared **advanced** engine actually exists on the engine (name/alias) — forwarding an undeclared one is a runtime binding error, invisible to parse/`STRUCTURE_OK`/`GATE_OK` and to `Test-XpzParameterNamingContract.ps1` (which checks the engine's own surface, not the wrapper→engine **consumption**). It generalizes the point heuristics `missing_AsJson_passthrough`/`consumes_legacy_text_stdout` (additive, not a replacement). Scope discriminator **by value**: transitive resolution (depth 1) of the `&` target down to `Join-Path … 'scripts\<Leaf>.ps1'` with the **literal `'scripts'` in the AST** (not the resolved path — `Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'X.ps1'` resolves to `…/scripts/X.ps1` but is a local sibling, out of scope) + leaf existing in the auditor's `scripts/`; a renamed root (`$MyBase` instead of `$SharedSkillsRoot`) is audited normally. **Advanced** detection without executing: an **injected** CommonParameter (present in `Get-Command.Parameters` but absent from the AST `param()` block) — a SIMPLE engine is permissive (`$args`) and is **skipped**. `OrdinalIgnoreCase` comparison; `Get-Command` does **not** throw on a broken engine (detection via `Parser::ParseFile`). Signals → `INVENTORY_CUSTOMIZED` (captured by the `Test-XpzSetupAudit.ps1` aggregator, **without touching the aggregator**): `forwards_unknown_engine_param` and `shared_engine_unresolved` (nonexistent engine path; consistent with 8.a.ii). A broken canonical engine → soft channel `INVENTORY_ENGINE_DIAGNOSTIC:` (label deliberately **outside** the four pendency tokens; does not block the setup state). **Known limits** (skip the site + follow-up in `999`): positional forwarding, `@PSBoundParameters`/dynamic root/multi-hop, `Invoke-Expression`/dot-source. New self-tests: `scripts/Test-XpzWrapperEngineParamSupportSelfTest.ps1` (13 unit cases) and `scripts/Test-XpzWrapperEngineParamMoldesSelfTest.ps1` (safety: **zero signals** over the real molds + non-vacuous coverage); `scripts/Test-XpzWrapperInventorySelfTest.ps1` extended (end-to-end case). Since `Test-XpzWrapperInventory.ps1` is in `xpz-kb-parallel-setup/setup-contract.manifest.json`, the change **alters the setup contract signature** and triggers re-audit. Documented in `xpz-kb-parallel-setup/SKILL.md` (8.a.ii, 8.h and the soft-channel note) and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`. Plan converged via **peer review** (12 versions, 4 families — native anthropic, openai/Codex, ollama-cloud deepseek/glm/kimi; the **by-value** discriminator was glm-5.2's finding, the aggregator-integration gap was gpt-5.5's finding).
- Migrated the `xpz-llm-delegate` opencode adapters from **argument-based** to **stdin-based**: `Invoke-OpenCode.ps1` and `Start-OpenCodeJob.ps1` now deliver the prompt **via stdin** (a file through `Start-Process -RedirectStandardInput`), out of the argv, dropping the temporary runner and mirroring `Invoke-Codex.ps1`/`Start-CodexJob.ps1`. This deterministically fixes the **~32KB Windows command-line limit** (`Argument list too long`) for large prompts and, by using **explicit redirection** of stdout/stderr to files, eliminates on opencode the non-deterministic `StandardOutputEncoding is only supported when standard output is redirected` error (observed symptom, unconfirmed cause — suspected `IsOutputRedirected=False`; see `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`). New additive parameters: `-MessagePath <file>` (reads the prompt from a file; ParameterSet exclusive with `-Message`; also avoids `"$(cat ...)"` on the caller's command line) and `-OpenCodeExe` (executable override, enables the self-test fake-exe). Stdin encoding UTF-8 without BOM (`Set-Content -Encoding utf8 -NoNewline`), temp cleanup adjusted, `Kill($true)` on timeout. **Headless anti-hang preserved** (the stdin file's EOF replaces the `$null |`). `Invoke-Gemini`/`Invoke-Copilot` stay **argument-based** (no subscription to validate stdin; only the limit is documented — follow-up in `999-ideias-pendentes.md`). Self-test `scripts/Test-LlmDelegateStdinHandlingSelfTest.ps1` extended: reclassifies opencode as stdin-based, guards that the prompt is not in the argv, and behaviorally proves a > 32KB prompt via stdin with a fake-exe; also validated empirically end-to-end (42KB prompt with pt-BR accents, real opencode, ~7s). Documented in `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md` (STDIN section rewritten for both regimes + new limit section) and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`. Plan converged via **peer review** (6 reviewers / 4 families; design converged at v2, resubmission declined with an audited record).
- Taught `scripts/Test-XpzWrapperInventory.ps1` to flag `INVENTORY_CUSTOMIZED` with reason **`consumes_legacy_text_stdout`** for the local wrapper `Update-*KbFromXpz.ps1` that still consumes the `Sync-GeneXusXpzToXml.ps1` engine stdout as **text** instead of the **JSON v1** contract. Closes the gap left by the sync JSON-contract front (a stdout breaking change): the skill that **audits** the local wrappers (`xpz-kb-parallel-setup`) got no matching check, so a textual `Update-*KbFromXpz.ps1` passed as **non-customized** (invisible to the setup gate). Text-vs-template heuristic identical to the `missing_AsJson_passthrough` precedent (the `Update-KbFromXpz.example.ps1` template consumes `ConvertFrom-Json`; it flags when the local one does not). **Known limit (conservative text heuristic):** it does not detect partial migration or alternative parsers (`System.Text.Json`), and it is **blind to version drift within the JSON** (a wrapper parsing v1 with `ConvertFrom-Json` would pass even with the engine at `SchemaVersion=2`) — that case stays in the contract-version follow-up (`Kind`/`SchemaVersion`) in `999-ideias-pendentes.md`. Because `Test-XpzWrapperInventory.ps1` is in `xpz-kb-parallel-setup/setup-contract.manifest.json`, the change **alters the setup contract signature** and triggers re-audit in parallel folders. Also added the **lockstep method rule** (isolated commit): `13-revisao-pre-push.md` (§3), the root `AGENTS.md`, and the mirror in `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md` now ask, in the semantic phase, whether a front that changes the **consumption contract of a shared engine** gave the skill that **audits the consumers** the drift check in the same PR. Self-test `scripts/Test-XpzWrapperInventorySelfTest.ps1` extended (textual wrapper → flagged; wrapper migrated to JSON v1 → not flagged). Documented in `xpz-kb-parallel-setup/SKILL.md` (8.a.ii, table 8.h, and the per-reason `INVENTORY_CUSTOMIZED` rule) and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`. Plan converged via **peer review** (6 reviewers / 4 families; v2 = hardening, resubmission declined with an audited record).
- The output contract of `scripts/Sync-GeneXusXpzToXml.ps1` (the XPZ→XML sync engine) is now **machine JSON on stdout** (`Kind=xpz-sync-result`, `-Compress`, one line), with all human text moved to **stderr** — a **breaking change** versus the previous textual `Format-List` (the only known consumer, the `Update-KbFromXpz.example.ps1` template, was already broken reading `$result.Field` off a string and was fixed). Empirical reason: in a child process (`pwsh -File`), `Write-Host`/`Write-Warning`/`Write-Information` **leak** into the captured stdout; only `[Console]::Error` stays out. The engine exposes ready **nominal lists** (removing the need for `git diff` to name objects): `CreatedNames`/`UpdatedNames`/`UnchangedNames`/`SkippedOlderLastUpdateNames` (`"Type:Name"`, `len==counter`), `RenamedByGuidItems` (`{Guid,FolderType,OldName,NewName,Action}`) and `ExpectedReturnedNames`/`ExpectedMissingNames`/`AdditionalOfficialNames` (flattened; source of the three-part handoff). The >10 presentation rule (per category) is agent behavior, documented in `xpz-sync/SKILL.md`. The full `$report` (with raw `Writes`/`Renames`) is still available via `-ReportPath`. Template fixed: consumes the JSON, `.Count`→`[int]` on `FullSnapshotMissing/Extra`, renames enter `hasMaterialChange`, diagnostics→stderr. Self-test `Test-XpzSyncGuidRenameSelfTest.ps1` extended (cases A–G: stdout purity, invariants, empty→`[]`, `orphan-removed`, `Expected*Names`). Parity: `xpz-sync/SKILL.md`, `08`, `09`, `README` (3 languages). Local `Update-*KbFromXpz.ps1` wrappers in parallel folders must be updated to consume JSON (derived front recorded in `999`). Design converged by a multi-model peer review (3 rounds + a shape decision; 6 reviewers / 3 families) and empirical verification.
- Hardened **Achado B** in `xpz-llm-delegate` (delegation to external models) after auditing a real case: an all-external composition **with no local reviewer** for a `kb-sensitive` payload is now a **conscious override** (only by explicit textual authorization; absence of a veto ≠ authorization), never a neutral co-equal option; the gate's `allow` answers "is it permitted?", not "is it prudent?", and does **not** dispense with the Achado B caution (`gate=deny` invalidates the override). New **proportional risk notice** before an external `kb-sensitive` send: **enumerate (do not label)** what leaves, the number + nominal list of destinations (each an independent disclosure) and material irreversibility, in the **same message** as the request, not compressed as "risk + ok?"; **per-destination consent**, no carry-over; receipt fields `riskNotice`/`override` (`humanAuthorizer` + `authorizationTextRef`, `localReviewerStatus`; `consciousOverride` requires a resolved status). **Forbidden to frame** the panel's cost/latency to shrink it, except on explicit human request. Doc-only (`xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`); validated by a two-round peer review (6 reviewers / 3 families).
- Mechanized the **vN+1 state axis** in peer review (Achado A of the 4-findings front): `scripts/Resolve-LlmDelegatePeerReviewCloseout.ps1` gained `-VNextState` (`notProduced`|`pendingResubmission`|`resubmitted`|`resubmissionDeclinedByHuman`, a string validated by `ValidateSet` — Achado C's contract, never `[bool]`) + `-ResubmissionDeclinedBy`/`-ResubmissionDeclineReason`. `pendingResubmission` blocks closeout (`vnext-pending-resubmission`, with `requiredUserPrompt` offering the 2nd round); `resubmissionDeclinedByHuman` blocks if who/why is missing (`vnext-resubmission-decline-unaudited`); `resubmitted`/`notProduced` are neutral; `vNextState` is **always echoed** in the `receiptAddendum` (including `notProduced`, so it is never silently absent). The entry trigger is the textual rule (the agent records `pendingResubmission` when **authoring** the vN+1) plus the same exit check — no second script. The closeout is **stateless**: monotonicity/append-only across calls belongs to the **orchestrator + receipt/ledger**, not the script (honest limit: proof against **silence**, not against **fabrication**). `15-revisao-por-pares.md` gained A1 ("agree with caveats"/"approved with suggestions" = non-convergence; the orchestrator reports and does not close), A3 (anchoring the rule at vN+1 authoring) and `vNextState` as a mandatory field of the minimal receipt. Self-test `scripts/Test-LlmDelegatePeerReviewCloseoutSelfTest.ps1` extended (cases `pendingResubmission`→blocks; decline without who/why→blocks; audited decline→releases+echoes; `resubmitted`→releases; `notProduced`→neutral+echoes). Parity: `15`, `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md` (SCRIPTS + step 3b), `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md` (pointer; owner = SKILL/15), `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`, `AGENTS.md` (minimal receipt). `13-revisao-pre-push.md` = justified dismissal (does not change the pre-push gate); `README.md` verified (already points to `15`, which carries the rule). Design converged in substance by a multi-model panel (v5→v8, 4 voices / 3 families: native anthropic, Codex, minimax, kimi); diff reviewed afterwards.
- Documented the kb-sensitive heuristic of the preferred list + discovery/bootstrap protocol (Achado B): note in `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md` — for a `kb-sensitive` payload, an **all-external** preferred composition raises a warning + asks the human for a **local** reviewer; with no local reviewer, **stop** (do not force external). Discovery protocol with `Get-Command`/probes only as a last-resort diagnostic (not the main path): order `capabilities.json` → `Resolve-*ModelLocality` → `opencode models`. **Bootstrap** when `capabilities.json` does not exist (virgin machine): no hard fail, no stub created, no fabricated signature — live probes for-this-round-only + opt-in offer of the manifest; **end of chain** → ask the human/stop; **human-silence fallback** → no answer ≠ authorization (stop/local-only + log the bypass). `capabilities.json` freshness is by the **`generatedAt`** field (not `updatedAt`, which belongs to `preferred-reviewers.json`); distinct timestamps per artifact recorded. Where each axis (capability≠signature≠preference≠authorization) is kept: distinct components, none collapses into another.
- Recorded the result of the **confirmatory sweep of the other adapters** (Achado D / G1-R, mandatory gate): truncation detection by `reason` is **opencode-only**; static inspection (2026-06-20) of the Codex/Claude Code/Gemini/Copilot adapters showed that the **D-leak** (a preamble becoming the verdict) **does not reproduce** (all deliver the canonical final message — a named terminal field; Copilot via stream *last-wins*), but **token-limit truncation is not detected** outside opencode. Per-adapter coverage stated in the «Truncation detection (Achado D)» section of `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`; residual limit + Copilot *last-wins* risk + empirical test plan recorded as a future front in `999-ideias-pendentes.md`.
- Hardened the peer-review methodology (`15-revisao-por-pares.md`) and its mechanism (`xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`) after dogfooding a real case: (1) the minimal prompt now **shields the reviewer's role** (emit a verdict; do not execute/conduct/delegate) — agentic backends had taken over the task instead of opining; (2) `responded` requires a **usable** verdict, an off-task/empty return is `noResponse` and does **not** count as a family "effectively consulted" toward convergence (post-hoc gate; the mechanical floor engine is pre-dispatch and stays untouched); (3) recovery via **single-flight retry** of an `ollama-cloud/*` reviewer that fails with no text in a concurrent batch, before closing `error`; (4) a new version generated from the verdicts (vN+1) **has not been reviewed yet** — declaring it "done" without re-submitting is false convergence; (5) "stop at the 1st gap" governs the **decision/convergence**, not the **dispatch mode**: in plan validation collection may be **parallel**, in pre-push dispatch is serial by **cost** (full `13` routine per reviewer; `14:34`). Doc-only changes in `15`, `14`, and `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`; the scripts' contract is unchanged.
- Strengthened the `preferred-reviewers.json` contract in peer review: when the list exists, it feeds the panel's preferred candidates and must not be treated as an optional pool from which the agent can stop at the minimum 2-family floor. The agent must run the gate per preferred reviewer, compute diversity over the complete preferred-candidate list + verdicts, and record each preferred reviewer's final state in the receipt. `Resolve-LlmDelegatePeerReviewCloseout.ps1` now blocks `closeoutReady` when a preferred list exists but auditable states are missing, or when a state is still incomplete (`gateAllow`, `dispatched`, `enqueued`). Preserved: preference does not replace authorization, role policy/vetoes, or ad-hoc human override.
- Limited operational parallelism for `ollama-cloud/*` reviewers in `xpz-llm-delegate` to 3 simultaneous calls per provider, queueing the rest. Why: in a real test, 4 Ollama Cloud models in parallel produced no useful review from one reviewer, while the same model answered normally when run alone; a no-text failure in that scenario should first be triaged as provider concurrency saturation, not as low model quality.
- Added mechanical peer-review closeout to `xpz-llm-delegate`: `scripts/Resolve-LlmDelegatePeerReviewCloseout.ps1` blocks the final receipt when a round started without `preferred-reviewers.json`, the user manually chose reviewers, and the agent has not yet offered to save that curation. The engine returns `closeoutReady`, `blockingReasons`, `requiredUserPrompt`, and `receiptAddendum`, without writing preferences, deciding authorization, or recalculating diversity. Self-test `scripts/Test-LlmDelegatePeerReviewCloseoutSelfTest.ps1`. Documented in `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`, `15-revisao-por-pares.md`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Strengthened the separation between per-KB authorization and preferred-reviewer curation in `xpz-llm-delegate`: after the user chooses reviewers for a round without `preferred-reviewers.json`, the agent must offer to save **that already-made selection** in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\xpz-llm-delegate\preferred-reviewers.json` via `Set-LlmDelegatePreferredReviewers.ps1`, as a decision separate from persisting `llm-delegation-policy.json`. Preference does not replace authorization, authorization does not create preference, and an ad-hoc override over an existing list must not overwrite the curation automatically.
- Added a mandatory format for the peer-review question when `preferred-reviewers.json` is absent: before sending any payload, the agent must state that the list is absent, state the payload class, ask which tools/models the user has and wants to use, filter examples already rejected in the conversation, and state that inventory/authorization are diagnostic, not preference. Recommending a composition at that point is forbidden (for example, "objective option", "simplest path", "I suggest X + Y") when an external reviewer lacks a registered preference.
- Blocked default recommendations of external reviewer compositions in `xpz-llm-delegate` when `preferred-reviewers.json` is absent: the agent must not suggest the "simplest path" with Gemini/Copilot/Codex cloud or another external service merely because the backend was detected or the gate returned `allow`/`ask`; it must calibrate which tools the user has and wants to use, respecting explicit conversational context. Also strengthened that the manuscript/prompt sent to reviewers must not embed as facts the conclusions the review is supposed to validate (for example, "universal", "self-generated sequential", "KB standard" without a normative source), separating observed evidence from the plan hypothesis.
- Strengthened the initial-question contract in `xpz-llm-delegate` for peer review without `preferred-reviewers.json`: the question must calibrate human preference and available subscription/login, not enumerate everything installed as a prescriptive menu. Detected backends without a registered preference must appear as "detected; confirm whether you want to use it", never as an implicit recommendation. Added the explicit four-axis framework for reviewer selection (detected capability, subscription/login, preference, per-KB authorization), with the rule that policy `allow-external`/`ask` decides confidentiality/sending only, never selects a reviewer or proves preference.
- Exposed the essential entry contract for skill consumers inside `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md` itself: on `revisão por pares`/`peer review`, the agent must load the skill, resolve or calibrate `preferred-reviewers.json`, ask for available tools in tool-language when no list exists, not assume external subscriptions, allow native subagents to participate without counting them as an external family, and only use the label with a valid panel + minimal receipt. The skill short description now exposes this contract too, because consumers do not necessarily read the development repository's `AGENTS.md`.
- Made the `preferred-reviewers.json` calibration trigger mandatory: on the first peer-review use without a preferred-reviewer list, the agent must ask which tools/models the user has available or prefers (`Claude Code`, `opencode/Ollama Cloud`, `Codex`, `Copilot`, `Gemini`, native subagent), instead of assuming an external subscription or offering Gemini/Copilot from generic enumeration. A native subagent may participate, but counts as the orchestrator's family and does not replace an external family for meeting the floor.
- Strengthened the operational contract for `revisão por pares`: beyond being a reserved term, the label now requires a **minimal receipt** (methodology files read, manuscript/prompt, effectively consulted reviewers, families, diversity floor, and verdicts) and records that an answer in under 30s from the request is incompatible with real peer review, except when reporting an identifiable previously completed panel. The file ledger remains optional; the response receipt is now mandatory to use the label.
- Formalized in the local `AGENTS.md` and in `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md` that `revisão por pares`/`peer review` is a reserved operational term: before answering, the agent must read `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md` and `15-revisao-por-pares.md` (plus `14`/`13` for reinforced pre-push), and must not label a solo opinion as peer review. Without a valid panel with >=2 effectively consulted families, the result must be called `parecer solo` or `segunda opinião (N)`.
- Made `xpz-llm-delegate` discovery more explicit: the skill frontmatter now includes the `revisão por pares`/`peer review`, `plan/design`, and `multi-model panel` triggers so agents receiving that request load the skill before answering. This does not change the `15` methodology; it only exposes in discovery metadata a use already documented under `TRIGGERS`.
- Strengthened the **diversity-floor** rule after another real test: the floor applies to the **entire panel composition**, whatever the mounting — in particular **native subagents count as the orchestrator's family** (e.g., `anthropic/*` when the orchestrator is Claude) and must be **injected as candidates** into `Resolve-LlmDelegatePanelDiversity`; a panel of only native subagents = 1 family = "second opinion", not peer review. Reaffirmed **independence** (same minimal prompt, **no assigned personas/lenses**; personas remain a future). Why: in a test, the agent mounted the panel with 2 native subagents (same family) + personas, **bypassing the floor** by not routing through `xpz-llm-delegate`. Methodology only (`15` rule/composition/roles, `14`, `08`, `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`, `09`); the helper did **not** change (the native case is already covered by injecting candidates). Converged by a multi-model panel.
- Added a mechanical **diversity floor** to peer review (`scripts/Resolve-LlmDelegatePanelDiversity.ps1`, consultive): a peer review requires **≥2 distinct families** (family = destination provider) among dispatchable reviewers; below that it **is not a panel**. Why (found in a real test, parallel folder, kb-sensitive payload): the diverse reviewers were external → gate `ask` → the agent **silently dropped the `ask` ones** and proceeded with the single pre-authorized one → a "peer review" with 1 voice, violating the `15`/`14` guardrail. The engine takes candidates + gate verdicts and returns `panelReady` / `needsBatchAuthorization` (present the `ask` ones **together** for batch authorization) / `insufficientDiversity` (label as **"second opinion (N)"**, never a panel); it is **consultive** (does not decide authorization — the gate is sovereign). `15` gained the floor **in the convergence rule and the composition**; the `xpz-llm-delegate` GUIDELINE now requires presenting `ask` in batch for the panel, never dropping. Self-test `scripts/Test-LlmDelegatePanelDiversitySelfTest.ps1`. Parity in `14`, `08`, `09`. Plan converged by a multi-model panel (Codex argued for the helper against "doc-rule is enough").
- Fixed a **delegation-gate crash** (`scripts/Resolve-LlmDelegateAuthorization.ps1`): with `-Backend claude-code` **without** `-Model` **and** a per-KB policy present (via `-PolicyPath`/`-ParallelKbRoot`), the gate threw `"value of argument 'name' is not valid"` instead of a verdict. Cause: the claude-code resolver returns `canonicalModel=null` when the model is omitted (no derivable default, unlike codex), so `targetModelKey` was empty and the policy lookup called `PSObject.Properties['']`, which throws. Fix: **fail-closed** short-circuit when the destination is unresolvable (empty `targetModelKey`) — `kb-sensitive → ask` (as `.PARAMETER Model` already documented), without consulting the policy; and `Get-Prop` hardened to return `null` for an empty name. Backends `codex`/`opencode`/`copilot`/`gemini` were unaffected. Self-test `scripts/Test-LlmDelegateAuthorizationSelfTest.ps1` gained the regression case. Bug found during a real peer-review-panel test in a parallel folder.
- Added a **preferred-reviewers list** (the user's curation) to `xpz-llm-delegate`, so the peer-review offer composes the panel automatically without re-asking. Why: the opencode model curation lives only in the internal DB (`opencode.db`) — not in `opencode.jsonc` (minimal config) nor in `opencode models` (the full 39-item catalog); depending on the DB would be brittle. **Machine-level** solution: `scripts/Set-LlmDelegatePreferredReviewers.ps1` persists `%LOCALAPPDATA%\xpz-llm-delegate\preferred-reviewers.json` (**two-axis schema** — `targetModelKey` for policy/authorization + sanitized `invokeArgs` for the adapter; **discards** the hard veto Mistral/Nemotron), and `scripts/Resolve-LlmDelegatePreferredReviewers.ps1` cross-references `capabilities.json` and returns the suggested composition (**preference ≠ authorization**: the `Resolve-LlmDelegateAuthorization` gate re-evaluates per reviewer). **Three distinct artifacts:** per-KB policy (authorization) ≠ `capabilities.json` (probe) ≠ `preferred-reviewers.json` (preference). Calibration is **offered, never automatic**, at 3 triggers: first use without a list (just-in-time), opt-in in `xpz-skills-setup`, recalibration on staleness. Self-test `scripts/Test-LlmDelegatePreferredReviewersSelfTest.ps1`. Plan converged by a multi-model panel (v1→v3). Documented in `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`, `15-revisao-por-pares.md`, `xpz-skills-setup/SKILL.md`, and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Revised the `README.md` **language-model policy** (trilingual): instead of a fixed list of vetoed "weak" versions (which went stale — it still named `Kimi K2.6`/`MiniMax M2.7` while newer versions were in use — and contradicted the practice recorded in the history), the policy now splits by **role**. A **solo model** (driving GeneXus triage/build or recommended as a user default) requires strong instruction adherence; a **voice in a peer-review panel** admits less-strong models as **additional voices**, never a sole-decisive reviewer (the objection still goes through human triage). **Hard veto** restricted to `Mistral Large 3` and `Nemotron 3 Ultra` (the only ones with proven low grounding — history/commit `33bd9aa`). Criterion = observed adherence, not name/version (dated, non-exhaustive examples). Reconciled `14-revisao-pre-push-reforcada.md`, `15-revisao-por-pares.md`, and `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md` to the role split. Revision validated by a multi-model panel (Codex, deepseek-v4-pro, native Claude).
- Formalized **peer review** as a generic methodology for validating a plan/design via a multi-model panel: new document `15-revisao-por-pares.md` (manuscript → independent panel of distinct models that read the sources → revise-and-resubmit → whole-panel convergence on the final version), made the **normative source of the convergence rule**. `14-revisao-pre-push-reforcada.md` became the **pre-push application** of that methodology (defers the rule and roles to `15`, keeping only the pre-push specifics). The `xpz-llm-delegate` skill gained the peer-review trigger and the engine `scripts/Build-LlmDelegateCapabilityManifest.ps1` — probes installed backends/models → **sanitized** machine-level capability manifest (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\xpz-llm-delegate\capabilities.json`; only `canonicalModel`/`backend`/`locality`/`reasonCode`/`sourceKind`, never token/key/baseURL/path) + optional per-KB snapshot in `Temp/`, an **offer hint, never gate truth** — with self-test `scripts/Test-LlmDelegateCapabilityManifestSelfTest.ps1`. `xpz-kb-parallel-setup` offers to write the snapshot at the (optional) delegation-policy moment and added the engine to `setup-contract.manifest.json`. Cross-referenced in `13`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`, `00-indice-da-base-genexus-xpz-xml.md`, `AGENTS.md`, and `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`.
- Taught `scripts/Test-XpzWrapperInventory.ps1` to flag `INVENTORY_CUSTOMIZED` with reason **`missing_AsJson_passthrough`** for the K8/K9 local wrappers `Test-*KbSetupAudit.ps1` and `Test-*KbIndexGate.ps1` when the canonical template (`xpz-kb-parallel-setup/examples/Test-KbSetupAudit.example.ps1` / `Test-KbIndexGate.example.ps1`) forwards `-AsJson` to the engine but the local wrapper does **not** forward it. Closes the divergence where an old local wrapper (without `-AsJson`) passed as `INVENTORY_OK` in the setup audit, while the KB parallel-folder pre-push routine (K8/K9 gates of the **`xpz-kb-parallel-pre-push`** skill) blocked it because it requires the structured contract. The check follows the pattern of the existing content block for `Set-KbSourceMetadataDeployment` (compares the template text against the local wrapper text). Because `Test-XpzWrapperInventory.ps1` is in `xpz-kb-parallel-setup/setup-contract.manifest.json`, this change alters the setup contract signature and triggers re-audit in parallel folders. Self-test `scripts/Test-XpzWrapperInventorySelfTest.ps1` extended (wrapper without `-AsJson` → flagged; wrapper with `-AsJson` → not flagged for this reason). Documented in `xpz-kb-parallel-setup/SKILL.md` and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Promoted the **KB parallel-folder** pre-push routine to the **`xpz-kb-parallel-pre-push`** skill (sibling of `xpz-kb-parallel-setup`), generalizing logic previously tied to a specific KB. Created the skill folder: a concise `SKILL.md` (structure of `xpz-sync`, reduced depth) + satellites `fase1-mecanica`/`fase2a-estrutural`/`fase2b-classificador-de-regime` + `agents/openai.yaml` + `examples/` (thin orchestrator wrapper, config with `layerTokens`, per-KB accepted-patterns catalog, report templates). On top of the shared-engine core already in `scripts/`: orchestrator `Invoke-XpzKbParallelPrePushPhase1.ps1` (G0–G5 + K1–K4/K8/K9/K11, machine JSON by default, exit 0 ready / 2 warn / 1 blocked, `unknown`⇒`blocked` fail-closed, K8/K9 wrapper discovery config→convention→fail-closed) and engines `Test-XpzKbDangerousPaths`, `Test-XpzKbLayerDiff`, `Test-XpzNotNotIsAntipattern`, `Test-XpzKbFrenteHygiene`, `Compare-XpzChecksums`, `Test-XpzKbIndexGate`. Added 8 self-tests (helper `scripts/XpzKbPrePushSelfTestSupport.ps1` that builds git fixture repos + UPPERCASE sentinels). It is a routine **distinct** from the skills-repository pre-push (document `13`); disambiguation added in `13`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, `14-revisao-pre-push-reforcada.md`, and `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`. `scripts/Test-XpzKbIndexGate.ps1` was added to `xpz-kb-parallel-setup/setup-contract.manifest.json` since it is a shared engine consumed via the K8/K9 path. Documented in `README.md` (trilingual), `AGENTS.md`, `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`, `13`, `14`, and `xpz-kb-parallel-setup/SKILL.md`.
- Fixed a **hang of the argument-based adapters** of `xpz-llm-delegate` (opencode, Gemini, Copilot) when invoked from a **headless shell without a TTY** (an agent's Bash/PowerShell tool): the temporary runner did not close the CLI's stdin, so the agentic CLI **hung** reading the inherited stdin (an open pipe that never reaches EOF). The 4 adapters — `Invoke-OpenCode.ps1`, `Start-OpenCodeJob.ps1`, `Invoke-Gemini.ps1`, `Invoke-Copilot.ps1` — now close stdin with `$null | & ([string]$req.exe) @args` (`$null` = pure EOF, **not** `''`, which would send an empty line); opencode used to hang for minutes and now completes in ~8s (sync and async proven from the headless shell). Deliberately **not** touched: `Invoke-Codex`/`Start-CodexJob` and `Invoke-ClaudeCode`/`Start-ClaudeCodeJob` deliver the prompt **via** stdin (`-RedirectStandardInput`); the `--version`/`--help` probes in the `*CliSupport` scripts were **measured** headless and do not hang (they do not read stdin); watchers and resolvers do not spawn a CLI. The fix depends on the runner being `pwsh -File`; if a CLI ever gains a `--stdin` mode, the closed stdin would break silently (caveat documented). New deterministic self-test `scripts/Test-LlmDelegateStdinHandlingSelfTest.ps1` (behavioral proof + static guard). Documented in `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md` and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`. **Later update (same `Unreleased`):** `Invoke-OpenCode.ps1`/`Start-OpenCodeJob.ps1` were subsequently migrated to **stdin-based** (prompt via stdin through `-RedirectStandardInput`, without `$null | &`) — see the migration entry at the top of this section; closing with `$null | &` remains **only** in the argument-based `Invoke-Gemini`/`Invoke-Copilot`.
- Strengthened the `xpz-llm-delegate` opencode backend for Ollama Cloud: `Resolve-OpenCodeModelLocality.ps1` now classifies known cloud providers (`ollama-cloud/*`, `opencode-go/*`) as `external` even without readable local config, avoiding `unknown` when the CLI uses `opencode.jsonc` or when config is unavailable. The skill documents that `-Model` must be `provider/model` (for example `ollama-cloud/deepseek-v4-pro`, not `deepseek-v4-pro`) and records operational recovery for CLI SQLite failures by backing up `opencode.db*`. Self-test `scripts/Test-OpenCodeModelLocalitySelfTest.ps1` covers the known cloud providers.
- Catalogued the named anti-pattern **`transaction-default-rule-with-conditional`** (`src0239`) in `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`: a `Transaction` `Default(<Attr>, <expr>)` rule does **not** accept a conditional clause (`... if <cond>;`) — the Specifier blocks it at import with `src0239: 'Default' rule does not support a conditional expression` (`confirmado-import`, reproduced on `OperacaoItem` in KB FabricaBrasil18 / environment NETPostgreSQL, `importTaskSuccess=false`). Idiomatic fix: keep the `Default` **unconditional** and move the condition to a separate assignment rule — **without** claiming runtime equivalence (`Default` = initial value when the attribute is empty; assignment = rule evaluation under its own trigger), a distinction that is GeneXus modeling knowledge (**nexa** boundary). Pointer in Catalog 3 of `xpz-builder/responsibilities-by-type/transaction.md` and a cross-ref to the «Cascade ordering» section as a **distinct mechanism**: `src0239` is **import rejection**, whereas the cascade `transaction-attribute-rule-shadowed-by-default-in-cascade` is **runtime shadowing** with clean import/build (do not confuse or merge).
- Added a **`-LogPath` fail-fast gate** (exit **50**, category `parametro-invalido`) to the 10 MSBuild wrappers: when the resolved `-LogPath` points to an **existing directory**, the wrapper blocks **early** — before opening the KB / registering the task — emitting the JSON block **to stdout only**, instead of running the whole operation and failing only at the final diagnostic write, which masked an actually-completed build/import as **exit 90** ("operational failure"). Key finding: the probe `Test-GeneXusMsBuildSetup.ps1` does **not** validate the wrapper's `-LogPath` (wrappers call the probe with an internal LogPath), so the gate runs in the wrapper itself against the real `-LogPath`. Shared pure engine `scripts/GeneXusMsBuildLogPathSupport.ps1` (`Get-GeneXusMsBuildLogPathRejection` + `New-GeneXusMsBuildLogPathBlockJson`); condition `Test-Path -PathType Container` (never `-not -PathType Leaf`, so a legitimate file-to-create with a valid parent is not blocked); it does not touch the JSON writing nor the local helpers each wrapper redefines. Wrappers covered: `Invoke-GeneXusKbBuildAll`, `Invoke-GeneXusXpzImport`, `Get-GeneXusKbProperty`, `Start-GeneXusKbBuildDetached` (preserves the file-to-create whose parent directory it creates itself), `Invoke-GeneXusXpzImportThenBuild`, `Invoke-GeneXusKbSpecifyGenerate`, `Invoke-GeneXusXpzExport`, `Test-GeneXusXpzImportPreview`, `Open-GeneXusKbHeadless`, `Test-GeneXusKbConsistency`. Self-test `scripts/Test-GeneXusMsBuildLogPathSupportSelfTest.ps1`; entry **50** in `scripts/msbuild-exit-codes.catalog.json` and `Start-GeneXusKbBuildDetached`/`Invoke-GeneXusXpzImportThenBuild` added to the `wrapperScanList` of `scripts/Test-MsBuildExitCodesCatalog.ps1`. Documented in `10-base-operacional-msbuild-headless.md`, `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`, `xpz-msbuild-build/SKILL.md`, and `xpz-msbuild-import-export/SKILL.md` (fixing the `-LogPath` example that pointed to what looked like a directory).
- Made the **front-not-opened** error message actionable and reinforced the open-via-`New-` prerequisite: the opaque `FrontFolder nao encontrado` `throw` in `scripts/Copy-GeneXusAcervoToFront.ps1` and in the drift gate `scripts/Test-GeneXusFrontAcervoDrift.ps1` gained the `FRENTE_NAO_ABERTA:` prefix and now instructs opening/resuming the front with `New-GeneXusXpzFront.ps1` (wrapper `New-*KbFront.ps1`, `-ReuseIfExists`) before populating, instead of nudging the agent to create the folder manually. The example `xpz-kb-parallel-setup/examples/Copy-KbAcervoToFront.example.ps1` now shows the 2-step flow (open the front → populate) and `.PARAMETER FrontFolder`/`FrontName` documents the precondition. Prerequisite also reinforced in `xpz-builder/SKILL.md` (9-FD gate), `xpz-builder/quality-checklist.md`, `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, and `README.md` (trilingual). Residue logged in `999-ideias-pendentes.md`: the same actionable message in the remaining 5 gates that receive `-FrontFolder` (duplicate vs. shared helper decision deferred). Self-test `scripts/Test-CopyGeneXusAcervoToFrontSelfTest.ps1` and parse OK.
- Fixed a bug in the delegation gate: `Resolve-LlmDelegateAuthorization.ps1 -Backend claude-code` **without** `-Model` failed with a binding error (`Cannot bind argument to parameter 'Model' because it is an empty string`) instead of the documented fail-safe behavior. Cause: `scripts/Resolve-ClaudeCodeModelLocality.ps1` had `-Model` as `Mandatory`. The resolver now accepts a missing `-Model` and returns `locality=unknown` (fail-closed: `kb-sensitive` payload → `ask`, `public` → `allow`), aligning with Copilot/Gemini/Codex and honoring the `.PARAMETER Model` help. Self-tests extended (`Test-ClaudeCodeModelLocalitySelfTest.ps1` empty `-Model` case; `Test-LlmDelegateAuthorizationSelfTest.ps1` claude-code without `-Model` cases). Gap found by a pre-push review delegated to Codex (gpt-5.5).
- Completed the doc↔engine parity of two contracts: the `Resolve-LlmDelegateAuthorization.ps1` gate signature in `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md` now lists `[-ConfigPath <opencode.json|config.toml>]` (a real parameter forwarded to the locality resolver, previously missing from the signature); and `xpz-builder/quality-checklist.md` gained the item to run `scripts/Test-GeneXusSourceSanity.ps1` before packaging (`sourceSanityStatus=fail` blocks), mirroring the rule already present in `xpz-builder/SKILL.md`. Documentation only; no engine change.
- Renamed the LLM delegation policy file to the canonical name **`llm-delegation-policy.json`**, with full backward compatibility to the legacy name `opencode-delegation-policy.json` (inherited from when only the opencode backend existed; the file governs all 5 backends by destination key, so a backend name was misleading). Added `scripts/Resolve-LlmDelegationPolicyPath.ps1`, which given the parallel-folder root resolves the effective path — canonical name with fallback to the legacy one — and returns `status` `new|legacy|both|none` + `deprecatedNameInUse` (path resolution only; it neither reads nor writes). The gate `scripts/Resolve-LlmDelegateAuthorization.ps1` gained `-ParallelKbRoot` (additive): with `-PolicyPath` omitted, it discovers the policy via that resolver and exposes `policyFileName`/`policyNameStatus`; an explicit `-PolicyPath` prevails — nothing breaks. `xpz-kb-parallel-setup` now writes with the canonical name and offers to rename the legacy one when present. The legacy name stays accepted **indefinitely**. Self-test `scripts/Test-LlmDelegationPolicyPathSelfTest.ps1` (4 states) and `-ParallelKbRoot` cases in `Test-LlmDelegateAuthorizationSelfTest.ps1`. Documented in `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`, `xpz-kb-parallel-setup/SKILL.md`, `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`, and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Closed the self-test coverage asymmetry of the `xpz-llm-delegate` backends: added `scripts/Test-CopilotCliSupportSelfTest.ps1` (deterministic JSONL stream parsing — `Get-CopilotJsonlFinalText`/`Get-CopilotJsonlExitCode` —, help contract and error extraction) and `scripts/Test-GeminiCliSupportSelfTest.ps1` (version parsing, help contract and error extraction), mirroring the CliSupport self-tests already present for Codex and Claude Code. The comment-based help of `.PARAMETER Model` in `scripts/Resolve-LlmDelegateAuthorization.ps1` now documents the behavior of an omitted `-Model` in the `claude-code` (locality `unknown` → fail-safe `ask` for sensitive payload), `copilot`, and `gemini` backends. No engine behavior change. Documented in `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Added the **GitHub Copilot CLI (#4)** and **Gemini CLI (#5)** backends to the `xpz-llm-delegate` skill, both synchronous and consultative (read-only), with the same destination-based policy-key philosophy as Codex. Copilot: `scripts/Invoke-Copilot.ps1` (`copilot -p` in JSONL — `--output-format json --stream off`, `--no-custom-instructions`, `--disable-builtin-mcps`, an empty tool set `--available-tools=` with `--allow-all-tools` made harmless since there is no tool to approve; the answer is extracted from the `assistant.message` event) and `scripts/CopilotCliSupport.ps1` (resolves the `copilot` command from PATH, minimum version `1.0.12`, validates the flag contract and reads the JSONL stream — `Get-CopilotJsonlFinalText`/`Get-CopilotJsonlExitCode`); the resolver `scripts/Resolve-CopilotModelLocality.ps1` emits the destination key `github-copilot/<model>` (default `gpt-5-mini`), deliberately **not** `openai/<model>`, because the operational destination is the GitHub Copilot service, not a direct call to the model's provider. Gemini: `scripts/Invoke-Gemini.ps1` (`gemini -p --approval-mode plan --output-format json`, only `plan` accepted, answer in the JSON `.response` field) and `scripts/GeminiCliSupport.ps1` (resolves the `gemini` command from PATH, minimum version `0.35.3`, validates the flag contract); the resolver `scripts/Resolve-GeminiModelLocality.ps1` emits `google/<model>` (default `gemini-3-flash-preview`). Both always `external`. The gate `scripts/Resolve-LlmDelegateAuthorization.ps1` gained `-Backend copilot|gemini` (additive) and matches the per-KB policy by the destination keys `github-copilot/*` and `google/*`. Deterministic self-tests `scripts/Test-CopilotModelLocalitySelfTest.ps1` and `scripts/Test-GeminiModelLocalitySelfTest.ps1`, plus `-Backend copilot|gemini` cases in `Test-LlmDelegateAuthorizationSelfTest.ps1`. Always human-triggered; GeneXus structural judgment is never delegated. Documented in `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`, `README.md` (trilingual), `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, `14-revisao-pre-push-reforcada.md`, and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Added the **Claude Code backend (#3)** to the `xpz-llm-delegate` skill (`claude -p`, prompt via stdin to avoid exposing the payload in a process argument, restricted short-query mode: no session persistence, `--permission-mode plan`, read-only tools `Read,Glob,Grep`; `PermissionMode=bypassPermissions` is rejected), synchronous and asynchronous: `scripts/Invoke-ClaudeCode.ps1` (`--output-format text`, final text answer), `scripts/Start-ClaudeCodeJob.ps1` + `scripts/Watch-ClaudeCodeJob.ps1` (detached job with `--output-format stream-json`, a follow-up window and `result.json`), and `scripts/ClaudeCodeCliSupport.ps1` (resolves `claude.exe` from PATH, minimum version `2.1.118`, validates the flag contract required by the adapter and classifies the job's final status). The locality resolver `scripts/Resolve-ClaudeCodeModelLocality.ps1` treats Claude Code as an **Anthropic** destination, always `external`, emitting the destination key `anthropic/<model>` (default `claude-opus-4-8`; the `opus` alias is normalized to `claude-opus-4-8`, other bare aliases stay `unknown` to avoid ambiguous policy). The gate `scripts/Resolve-LlmDelegateAuthorization.ps1` gained `-Backend claude-code` (additive) and matches the per-KB policy by the destination key `anthropic/*`, never by the adapter. Deterministic self-tests `scripts/Test-ClaudeCodeModelLocalitySelfTest.ps1` and `scripts/Test-ClaudeCodeCliSupportSelfTest.ps1`, plus `-Backend claude-code` cases in `Test-LlmDelegateAuthorizationSelfTest.ps1`. Always human-triggered; GeneXus structural judgment is never delegated. Documented in `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`, `README.md` (trilingual), `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, `14-revisao-pre-push-reforcada.md`, and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Added the **Codex backend (#2)** to the `xpz-llm-delegate` skill (`codex exec`, fixed `read-only` sandbox, using the tool/config default when `-Model` is omitted), synchronous and asynchronous: `scripts/Invoke-Codex.ps1` (prompt via stdin, answer from `output-last-message`), `scripts/Start-CodexJob.ps1` + `scripts/Watch-CodexJob.ps1` (non-blocking job with a follow-up window and `result.json`), and `scripts/CodexCliSupport.ps1` (**fail-closed** discovery of the desktop-app `codex.exe` under `%LOCALAPPDATA%\OpenAI\Codex\bin` by highest version — ignoring the PATH npm shim, which the server rejects for GPT-5.5). The locality resolver `scripts/Resolve-CodexModelLocality.ps1` derives the **destination** provider from the Codex `config.toml` (`model`, `model_providers`/`profiles`/`base_url`) or from the `--oss`/`--local-provider` flags, emitting `canonicalModel` (e.g., `openai/gpt-5.5`). The gate `scripts/Resolve-LlmDelegateAuthorization.ps1` gained `-Backend opencode|codex` (default `opencode`, backward-compatible) and now matches the per-KB policy by the **destination key**, not by the adapter: Codex with an explicit OpenAI model or config default is governed by the same `openai/*` entries as opencode — never by a `codex/` prefix, whose absence avoids the hole where `openai/*: deny-external` would let Codex through. The design decision (the confidentiality axis is the **data destination**, not the engine that sends it) is consolidated in the new `ANATOMIA` section of the `SKILL.md`. Known limit: `codex exec` is agentic (inherits the `AGENTS.md`, ~50k input tokens, can run read-only commands) — it is not one-shot, and `read-only` does not bypass the gate. Deterministic self-tests `scripts/Test-CodexModelLocalitySelfTest.ps1` and `scripts/Test-CodexCliSupportSelfTest.ps1`, plus `-Backend codex` cases in `Test-LlmDelegateAuthorizationSelfTest.ps1` proving the destination-based unification. Documented in `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`, `README.md` (trilingual), `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Hardened the **resilient post-processing** of the build-all (`scripts/Invoke-GeneXusKbBuildAll.ps1`): when the diagnostic comes out partial after a clean `MSBuild` (serialization failure or outer recovery), the slim objects now carry `buildSignals` — signals already computed on the normal path, so the agent does **not** reopen the raw log: `ReorgDetected`, `ErrorCount`, and `PostBuildEvents` (the post-build window lines, the deploy `.bat` included, by its real name — no dedicated detector). `buildSignals.Complete=true` means the bucket is whole and trustworthy (do not reopen the log for those signals); `Complete=false` means partial, and a `null` field is **not** `0`/`[]` (not computed). Design decision: carry forward what was already computed instead of re-detecting on the degraded path (avoids a detector that diverges between the clean and degraded paths); deploy comes in as a generic post-build line, not as a boolean keyed on a `.bat` name. Documented in `xpz-msbuild-build/SKILL.md`, `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`, and `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`.
- Hardened the **wait contract** of the detached build mode (`xpz-msbuild-build`): the consumer must not poll the sentinel alone — a hard kill / OOM / `-ExecutionTimeLimit` expiry of the Scheduled Task before the `finally` would leave the sentinel unwritten and the wait stuck forever. Added the helper `scripts/Wait-GeneXusKbBuildDetached.ps1` combining **two signals** — sentinel (`done=true`) and the task **heartbeat** via `Get-ScheduledTask` (`State`) — with a race-grace window and timeout, returning `outcome` `concluido`/`falha-anomala`/`timeout` (exit 0/70/71); the orchestrator's `pollHint` and the detached-mode subsection in `SKILL.md` now point to this combined wait. Also documented the **logoff limitation** (the `LogonType Interactive` task survives closing the window/app but **not** logoff/reboot) and the conscious decision **not** to offer the `S4U`/Password mode (would require managing credentials; the build depends on the user's interactive context — GeneXus, mapped drives, profile). Self-test `scripts/Test-WaitGeneXusKbBuildDetachedContract.ps1`. No change to the approved mechanics (atomic sentinel, auto-removal, gates in the wrapper, opt-in with the visible window as default). Documented in `xpz-msbuild-build/SKILL.md`, `README.md` (trilingual), `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, `10-base-operacional-msbuild-headless.md`, and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Added the **opt-in detached mode** for long builds in `xpz-msbuild-build` (`scripts/Start-GeneXusKbBuildDetached.ps1`): an orchestrator that registers a one-shot **Scheduled Task** (hidden console via `-WindowStyle Hidden`) running `Invoke-GeneXusKbBuildAll.ps1` **outside the agent session** — under the Task Scheduler service, it survives closing the window or the app, which previously killed a long `BuildAll` (~1h) through a shared console and the `run_in_background` Job Object. Completion is signaled by a **sentinel file** (`{ done, exitCode, logPath, logExists, error, stdoutPath, stderrPath, finishedAt }`, atomic write) that the agent polls without keeping a window open. It is transport, not policy authority (the reorg/rebuild/costly-options gates stay in the wrapper); it forwards parameters via **hashtable splatting** (array splatting misaligned the name-value binding); v1 has no watcher (`timing.phases` empty). The skill's monitoring gate moved from *visible watcher required* to **readable monitoring required** — visible window (default) **or** opt-in detached mode with `msbuild.stdout.log` + sentinel —, with the detached mode being a conscious user decision under the agent's advice for long/background builds. `scripts/Watch-GeneXusMsBuildLog.ps1` got an anti-accident palliative (window title + `NÃO FECHAR` warning). Validated in a real build (FabricaBrasil18/NETPostgreSQL, running under `svchost`, outside the session). Self-test `scripts/Test-StartGeneXusKbBuildDetachedContract.ps1`; own exit codes (0, 46, 90) in `scripts/msbuild-exit-codes.catalog.json`. Documented in `xpz-msbuild-build/SKILL.md`, `README.md` (trilingual), `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, `10-base-operacional-msbuild-headless.md`, and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Added to the Source sanity gate (`scripts/Test-GeneXusSourceSanity.ps1`) the finding `procedural-in-conditions` (`fail`), type-aware: a **Procedure** whose Conditions part (`763f0d8b-...`) is non-empty is blocked before packaging, because a Procedure has no Conditions filter (predicates live in `For Each ... Where`) and code there causes the import error `src0055` ("Missed ';' at the end of the condition"). Evidence: `confirmado-import` (GeneXus 18 U13 + WorkWithPlus_Web 16.0.3.1) + `confirmado-acervo` (0/thousands of Procedures in FabricaBrasil). The rule uses an extensible `objectType→forbidden-parts` map (only Procedure today); WebPanel/Prompt/Selection List, where Conditions is a legitimate filter, are out of scope. Self-test `scripts/Test-GeneXusSourceSanitySelfTest.ps1` (6 cases). Documented in `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md` and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Added to the Transaction coherence gate (`scripts/Test-GeneXusTransactionCoherence.ps1`) the finding `wwp-screen-code-on-non-generated-transaction` (`fail`): a `Transaction` with `GenerateObject=False` (structure/table or Business Component, with no screen of its own generated) that still carries WorkWithPlus DVelop screen code in the `Events`/`Rules` parts — `Call("LoadWWPContext")`/`Call("<Trn>WW")` — is blocked before packaging, because that code references runtime that is not generated and the import fails with `src0246`/`src0294` (`confirmado-import`, GeneXus 18 U13 + WorkWithPlus_Web 16.0.3.1). The causal signal is the orphan call; `Apply:<wwp-guid>=True` and the `Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern` marker are corroborating, and the native Work With (`Apply:78cecefe`) is explicitly out of scope. New read of the Object-level `<Properties>` for `GenerateObject`; self-test `scripts/Test-GeneXusTransactionCoherenceSelfTest.ps1`. Documented in `xpz-builder/responsibilities-by-type/transaction.md` (Catalog 3 + checklist) and `xpz-builder/wwp-packaging.md` (distinguished from the inverse gap of missing derivatives + the WWP Theme prerequisite, which stays outside the gate).
- Made discoverable the output contract of the Source sanity gate: `scripts/Test-GeneXusSourceSanity.ps1` emits JSON by default and does not accept `-AsJson` (enforced by `Test-XpzParameterNamingContract.ps1`) — pre-existing behavior, now made explicit via comment-based help (`.SYNOPSIS`/`.OUTPUTS`, visible in `Get-Help`) and via a sub-bullet in `xpz-kb-parallel-setup/SKILL.md` warning local-wrapper authors not to forward `-AsJson` or a folder to the shared engine.
- Added **CSS class catalog and traceability** to the KbIntelligence index (`schema_version` `2`→`3`, `EXTRACTOR_SIGNATURE_VERSION` `5`→`6`; index rebuild expected across all parallel folders). Layer 1: `scripts/Build-KbIntelligenceIndex.py` catalogs classes in the new `css_class` table (dimensions `model` `legacy-theme` from `ThemeClass` via the `Name` property, never the corrupted stem like `:hover` / `design-system` from SCSS in the Styles Part `c6b14574-...` of `DesignSystem`; `origin` `kb-authored`/`packaged-module` from imported libs; `parent_class` for inheritance; `UNIQUE(model, class_name, defining_object_name)` that deliberately does not collapse across models). Layer 2: usage reuses `relations`+`evidence` (relations `uses_css_class` resolvable with `evidence_role` `css_layout`/`css_event`, and `uses_css_class_dynamic` for `.Class=` assignment by variable/`Format()`), covering the 7 matrix forms, the layout vectors `class`/`cellClass`/`rowClass`/`formClass` (not the numeric `gxObjClass`) and ignoring `//` and `/* */` comments; locator = object + snippet. New queries `css-classes` (catalog; default view only `kb-authored`, `case-sensitive` name lookup never filters origin, `legacy-theme` flagged deprecated) and `css-class-usage` (usage; declares honest coverage `dynamic_uses_total`/`found_in_catalog` and lists used-but-uncatalogued) in `scripts/Query-KbIntelligenceIndex.py` and the `.ps1` wrapper (`-Model`/`-Origin`/`-IncludeImported`). Self-test `scripts/Test-KbIntelligenceCssClassExtractionSelfTest.ps1`. Documented in `README.md` (trilingual), `scripts/README-kb-intelligence.md`, `xpz-index-triage/SKILL.md`, `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, and `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`.
- Added the **reinforced, optional** tier of the pre-push review (`14-revisao-pre-push-reforcada.md`): orchestration of a **diverse multi-model panel** on top of the `13` routine, with a convergence rule — **push-ready only when, after the last repository change, the whole panel reports "no gap" on that final state** (every new commit makes prior verdicts *stale*; return to the reviewer that objected). It separates three roles: **mechanical panel assembly** (dispatching diverse reviewers with the minimal verbatim prompt `execute a rotina pre push, sem push`, sequentially, pausing at the 1st gap) — which an agent may do under human triggering; **judgment nodes** (gap triage, convergence, declaring push-ready) — human only; and **being a reviewer** (the `13` routine). Prohibition: an agent never occupies the judgment nodes nor pretends a solo run is the reinforced review. The delegation mechanism lives in `xpz-llm-delegate`; the policy, in `14`. Cross-referenced in `00-indice-da-base-genexus-xpz-xml.md`, `13-revisao-pre-push.md`, `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, `AGENTS.md`, and `xpz-llm-delegate/SKILL.md`.
- Extended the `xpz-skills-setup` skill to manage the external `nexa` skill (the only named exception to the rule of not registering skills from other repositories). New `scripts/Initialize-NexaRepoGit.ps1` bootstraps the repository hosting `nexa` (`genexuslabs/genexus-skills`): it detects an existing clone via the target of any global `nexa` link, **clones** the official repo when missing (a capability the XPZ repo's own bootstrap deliberately forbids), checks the official `origin` while tolerating extra remotes (e.g. a personal `fork`), and blocks overwriting a non-empty folder without `.git` (`NEXA_DIR_NOT_REPO`); labels `NEXA_ALREADY_LINKED`/`NEXA_ORIGIN_ADDED`/`NEXA_REPO_CLONED`/`NEXA_REMOTE_MISMATCH`, with self-test `Test-NexaRepoGitSelfTest.ps1`. The `scripts/Test-XpzSkillsRegistration.ps1` engine now also classifies `nexa` in a separate section (`externalSkills`/`externalOverall` `EXTERNAL_SKILLS_OK`/`EXTERNAL_SKILLS_GAPS`, independent of `overall`), applying the same classification and the compact strategy; only `nexa` is managed by name — other skills in the external repo (e.g. `gx-sap`) stay dormant. Documented in `xpz-skills-setup/SKILL.md` (new `SKILL EXTERNA GERENCIADA: NEXA` section, steps 2–5 and TRIGGERS) and `README.md` (trilingual).
- Extended the `xpz-skills-setup` skill with repository setup and mechanized auditing, across three fronts. (1) **Git bootstrap** of a folder downloaded as a ZIP from GitHub (`scripts/Initialize-XpzSkillsRepoGit.ps1`): detects Git and, if missing, installs it via `winget` (`Git.Git`); runs `git init` + official remote (`https://github.com/GxBrasilNOficial/GeneXus-XPZ-Skills.git`) + `fetch` + `reset --mixed origin/main`, linking the official history without overwriting the ZIP files, with an anti-destructive gate (`GIT_LINKED_WITH_DRIFT` only aligns the working tree via `-AlignToOfficial` after confirmation); an empty folder (clone) remains a documented prerequisite. (2) **Registration audit engine** (`scripts/Test-XpzSkillsRegistration.ps1`), read-only, classifying each skill × tool (`OK` / `coberta_por_compatibilidade` / `ausente` / `órfã` / `quebrada`, honoring Codex's two scopes, OpenCode's native requirement and Cursor's compatibility), detecting orphans and reporting the Cursor global MCP freshness by hash (`MCP_OK` / `MCP_SERVER_STALE` / `MCP_CONFIG_INVALID` / `MCP_NOT_INSTALLED`); `overall` `REGISTRATION_OK`/`REGISTRATION_GAPS`. (3) **Global-instructions audit engine** (`scripts/Test-XpzGlobalInstructions.ps1` + contract `scripts/xpz-global-instructions-topics.psd1`): resolves the effective source per tool (follows `@<path>`, OpenCode `instructions[]` and the Cursor MCP `agentsPath`) and flags coverage of the minimum topics conservatively (`presente`/`nao_detectado`, never "ausente"); `overall` `GLOBAL_INSTRUCTIONS_OK`/`GLOBAL_INSTRUCTIONS_REVIEW`. The engines neither create/remove links nor write instructions: resolution actions remain manual, under explicit confirmation. Deterministic self-tests for each engine, including contract↔`SKILL.md` parity (anti-drift). Documented in `xpz-skills-setup/SKILL.md` (step 0, steps 2–4 and step 9) and `README.md` (trilingual).
- Added the `xpz-llm-delegate` skill: delegates smaller tasks or a second opinion to a secondary LLM via opencode (backend #1), **always human-triggered**, with deterministic local/external classification from the opencode config `baseURL` (`scripts/Resolve-OpenCodeModelLocality.ps1`) and an `allow`/`ask`/`deny` confidentiality gate (`scripts/Resolve-LlmDelegateAuthorization.ps1`) that combines payload sensitivity (`kb-sensitive`/`public`) with durable per-KB authorization in `opencode-delegation-policy.json` (fine-grained per `provider/model`). Parallel-folder content only reaches an external model with authorization; GeneXus structural judgment is never delegated. opencode backend scripts (`Invoke-OpenCode.ps1`, `Start-OpenCodeJob.ps1`, `Watch-OpenCodeJob.ps1`) migrated to `scripts/`. Documented in `README.md` (trilingual), `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`, and `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`.
- MSBuild post-build events no longer downgrade the build status blindly: `scripts/GeneXusMsBuildPostBuildEventsSupport.ps1` now classifies each event against a per-environment registered set (`kb_environment_post_build_event_hashes` in `kb-source-metadata.md`) — a registered event is expected (no downgrade), an unregistered one downgrades out of caution, a `REM` line is inert, and with no registry a sound player is recognized as benign; `scripts/Invoke-GeneXusKbBuildAll.ps1` and `scripts/Invoke-GeneXusKbSpecifyGenerate.ps1` expose `stdoutSignals.postBuildEventClassification`. Added the public script `scripts/Register-GeneXusKbPostBuildEvents.ps1` (author of the new field, under `xpz-kb-parallel-setup`), which registers events from a build's JSON, with confirmation by an exact phrase (interactive) or `-ConfirmRegistration` (agent). The deploy validation gate (`-PostImportDeployValidation`/`-StrictDeployBinCheck`) now decides by factual operational success (`exitCode` 0 + build completion marker), not by the status string, so a benign post-build event (build-finished chime) no longer suppresses the requested validation. `scripts/Test-GeneXusDeployBinFreshness.ps1` gained `-BuildResultJsonPath`, which derives the cutoff from the build's `timing.msbuildStart` and removes the manual `-BuildStartedAt` (explicit one prevails). Self-tests: event classification, registration round-trip, gate policy, and `-BuildStartedAt` resolution.
- Added classic `WebPanel` support to diagnosis and editing: `scripts/Get-GeneXusObjectSummary.ps1` gained the `webpanel` block (tables with `tableType` Flex/Responsive and `depth`, controls, buttons in both `<action>` and `<ucw>` Button forms, `eventNames`, and a `coverage` block reporting unknown `gxControlType` in `unknownUcwControlTypes`), resolved by the versioned catalog `scripts/gx-ucw-gxcontroltype-catalog.json` (documented in `04b-ucw-gxcontroltype-reference.md`); added `scripts/Add-GeneXusButton.ps1`, which inserts a button into a `WebPanel` surgically (form `action`/`ucw`, before or after a named leaf control's cell in a `Flex` table — `-BeforeControlName`/`-AfterControlName` anchors, mutually exclusive via parameter sets —, with an `Event` stub, `lastUpdate` bump, and well-formedness validation, reusing `GeneXusXmlSurgicalEditSupport.ps1` whose `Invoke-GeneXusXmlLiteralPatch` primitive gained the `InsertBefore` mode), with a fail-closed `RESPONSIVE_UNSAFE` gate on a `Responsive` table with populated `responsiveSizes`; documented in `xpz-builder/responsibilities-by-type/webpanel.md` the interpretive button rule (single declaration + On Click Event; disambiguation between layout button, WorkWith pattern `<actions>`, and `.Visible`/`.Icon` references). Contract suites `scripts/Test-GeneXusWebPanelShapeContract.ps1` and `scripts/Test-GeneXusAddButtonContract.ps1`.
- Demarcated the scope boundary in `xpz-msbuild-import-export/SKILL.md` (editing an object's `Source`/`Rules`/`CDATA` belongs to `xpz-builder`), with the discarded alternative recorded in `998-ideias-descartadas-e-porque.md`; `scripts/Compare-GeneXusPanelShape.ps1` now points to the `webpanel` block when given a `WebPanel` (it stays Panel-only), with the WebPanel extension recorded as an idea in `999-ideias-pendentes.md`.
- Made the front-based packaging drift gate (9-FD) of `scripts/New-XpzImportPackage.ps1` fail-closed: the gate now **always** runs before packaging; `-AcervoPath` became optional and, when omitted, the canonical acervo `<RepoRoot>/ObjetosDaKbEmXml` is auto-resolved; with no acervo resolvable, packaging is blocked, and the JSON output gained the `acervoResolvedBy` field (`explicit`/`convention`). Added the shared engine `scripts/Set-GeneXusXmlLastUpdate.ps1` (with a self-test) to re-stamp the `lastUpdate` of an already-edited front XML, in-place, without copying from the acervo or applying a delta. Documented in `xpz-msbuild-import-export/SKILL.md` the named footgun of the no-op import caused by a `lastUpdate` older than or equal to the live KB object (accepted without error, no effect).
- Hardened the consultative new-token propagation gate of the pre-push review (`scripts/Test-PrePushNewTokenPropagation.ps1`): each candidate gets a `mentionClass` (`prose`/`param-list-item`/`param-table-cell`/`command-example`); class-aware truncation (only `prose` honors `-MaxFindings`, non-prose candidates are never truncated); the orchestrator segregates non-prose into `nonProseVerdictRequired` and requires an item-by-item ledger in the `agentSemanticChecklist` (no collective justification), recommending a second pass by a distinct model above the non-prose threshold. The `13-revisao-pre-push.md` model-diversity safeguard was promoted from conditional reserve to recommended-above-threshold. The limit found (forcing a single-model reviewer hits a rationalization ceiling) is recorded in `998-ideias-descartadas-e-porque.md`.
- Unified the parameter-naming contract across XPZ wrappers/engines: canonical `-ObjectList` (alias `-ObjectNames`) for selection by name, with `-ObjectList` accepting `[string[]]` in export; `-InputPath` (alias `-Path`) for the primary input; and, in the import family (`Invoke-GeneXusXpzImport`, `Invoke-GeneXusXpzImportThenBuild`, `Test-GeneXusXpzImportPreview`), canonical `-InputPath` with aliases `-XpzPath` and `-Path`. The output JSON `XpzPath` key and the export output `-XpzPath` were preserved (direction rule). Additive aliases, no breakage. Added the deterministic guard `scripts/Test-XpzParameterNamingContract.ps1` and documented the canonical vocabulary in `02-regras-operacionais-e-runtime.md`.
- Added a consultative gate-enumeration parity gate to the pre-push review (`scripts/Test-PrePushGateEnumerationParity.ps1`): it derives the gates actually run by the orchestrator and flags enumerations in the routine documentation that became a proper subset; generalizes the propagation rule to "enumerated set" and covers the direction blind spot (an enumeration that names only the old members). Includes a self-test.
- Added a consultative traceability-placeholder gate to the pre-push review (`scripts/Test-PrePushHistoryCommitPlaceholder.ps1`): when the diff touches `historico/*.md`, it flags a `Commit:`/`PR:` field left with a generic placeholder (`este commit`, `TODO`, etc.) instead of the real hash; wired into the orchestrator as a warning, with a self-test.
- Added a consultative cross-skill coverage gate to the pre-push review (`scripts/Test-PrePushSharedScriptSkillCoverage.ps1`): when the diff changes a shared script, it lists the `SKILL.md`/`quality-checklist.md` files that document it and were not touched; wired into the orchestrator as a warning, with a self-test.
- Added a consultative new-token propagation gate to the pre-push review (`scripts/Test-PrePushNewTokenPropagation.ps1`): when the diff introduces a parameter/alias via a co-located transition, it lists repository mentions left with the old set; wired into the orchestrator as a warning, with a self-test.
- Strengthened the pre-push semantic phase to search for newly added contract terms and report candidate traceability gaps separately from the verdict.
- Standardized shared XPZ packaging, inventory, and sanity wrappers to emit JSON by default on stdout, without `-AsJson`, with structured blocks and operational `-InputPath`/`-ObjectList` aliases.
- Added a gate for costly MSBuild options: `CompileMains=true` and `DetailedNavigation=true` now require `-AllowCostlyBuildOptions` with explicit confirmation.
- Aligned discovery and traceability of governance documents in `README.md`, `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`, and `historico/IdeiasImplementadas_202606.md`.
- Aligned `08-guia-para-agente-gpt.md`, `09-inventario-e-rastreabilidade-publica.md`, and `999-ideias-pendentes.md` after the new changelog rule in pre-push review.
- Added mandatory semantic evaluation of `CHANGELOG.md` to the pre-push routine for changes with public impact.
- Documented in the contribution guide that pre-push review should evaluate updates to `CHANGELOG.md`.
- Added public governance documents: `SECURITY.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, `CODE_OF_CONDUCT.md`, and `CHANGELOG.md`.
